max_age=SESSION_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60,\n        expires=expires_at,\n        path=\"/\",\n        domain=\".ndsp.app\",\n    )\n\n\ndef clear_session_cookie(response: Response) -> None:\n    response.delete_cookie(\n        key=COOKIE_NAME,\n        path=\"/\",\n        domain=\".ndsp.app\",\n        secure=True,\n        samesite=\"none\",\n    )\n\n\ndef init_schema() -> None:\n    with email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n                name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',\n                status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',\n                created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n                updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n            );\n            \"\"\")\n\n            cur.execute(\"\"\"\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ndsp_subscriptions (\n                id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,\n                user_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES ndsp_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                plan TEXT NOT NULL trial_expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n                created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n                updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n            );\n            \"\"\")\n\n            cur.execute(\"\"\"\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ndsp_sessions (\n                id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,\n                user_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES ndsp_users(id) ON max_age=SESSION_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60,\n        expires=expires_at,\n        path=\"/\",\n        domain=\".ndsp.app\",\n    )\n\n\ndef clear_session_cookie(response: Response) -> None:\n    response.delete_cookie(\n        key=COOKIE_NAME,\n        path=\"/\",\n        domain=\".ndsp.app\",\n        secure=True,\n        samesite=\"none\",\n    )\n\n\ndef init_schema() -> None:\n    with email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n                name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',\n                status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',\n                created_at         cur.execute(\"\"\"\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ndsp_sessions (\n                id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,\n                user_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES ndsp_users(id) ON NOT EXISTS ndsp_subscriptions (\n                id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,\n                user_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES ndsp_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                plan TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'elite',\n                status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'trial',\n                source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'elite_trial',\n                trial_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n                trial_expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n                created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n                updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n            );\n            \"\"\")\n\n            cur.execute(\"\"\"\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ndsp_sessions (\n                id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,\n                user_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES ndsp_users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                token_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n                status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',\n                created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n                expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n                last_seen_at TIMESTAMPTZ\n            );\n            \"\"\")\n\n"}}
